<a id="pm-s1"></a>
## 1. Abstract and result contract

> We prove that radially aligned segment sweeps admit finite cylinder covers with one triangular base for each interval of any tagged partition. As the mesh tends to zero, the total perpendicular base area converges to a weighted parameter area. An explicit application covers every regular tetrahedron with total base area below half its minimum projection area, giving negative answers to the half-area question and the directionwise normalized 1-Codimensional Cylinder Covering Conjecture.

- **claim-radial-cover** (theorem, [source](#stmt-thm-radial)): For the data in <a href="#eq:radial-sweep" data-reference-type="eqref" data-reference="eq:radial-sweep">[eq:radial-sweep]</a>, suppose that $$w'(t)=\lambda(t)(a+tb)\qquad(t\in J)$$ for a continuous real function $\lambda$. For any finite partition $\alpha=t_0<t_1<\cdots<t_N=\beta$, put $I_i=[t_{i-1},t_i]$ and choose arbitrary tags $c_i\in I_i$. There are $N$ cylinders covering $\mathcal S$, with axes parallel to $e+w(c_i)$ and nondegenerate compact triangular perpendicular bases. These covers can be chosen so that their total base area tends to $\mathcal A$ as the mesh $\delta=\max_i\|I_i\|$ tends to zero, uniformly over the partitions and tags.* *Alternatively, take each base to be the perpendicular projection of the actual compact sweep with $t\in I_i$, in the same frozen direction $e+w(c_i)$. These $N$ cylinders cover $\mathcal S$, and their total base area also tends to $\mathcal A$, uniformly over the partitions and tags.
  - Explanation: For a radially aligned sweep, any tagged partition yields one cylinder per interval with a triangular base. The total base area converges to the weighted parameter area as the mesh shrinks. The same limit holds for the actual projected sweeps.
- **claim-tetrahedron-below-half** (construction, [source](#stmt-cor-tetrahedron)): Every regular tetrahedron $K\subset\mathbb R^3$ of positive edge length has a finite cylinder cover $B_i+\mathbb Ru_i$, with unit axes $u_i$ and nondegenerate compact triangular bases $B_i\subset u_i^\perp$, such that $$\sum_i\operatorname{area}(B_i)<\frac{A_{\min}(K)}2, \qquad \sum_i\frac{\operatorname{area}(B_i)}{\operatorname{area}(\pi_{u_i^\perp}K)}<\frac12.$$
  - Explanation: Every regular tetrahedron has a finite triangular-base cylinder cover whose total area is below half the minimum projection area. Its directionwise normalized cost is also below one half, which answers both questions negatively.
- **claim-crossing-lemma** (theorem, [source](#stmt-lem-crossing)): If $F\in C^1([a,b])$, $a<b$, and $F(a)\le z\le F(b)$, there is $r\in[a,b]$ with $F(r)=z$ and $F'(r)\ge0$, using one-sided derivatives at the endpoints.
  - Explanation: A continuously differentiable function that brackets a level attains it at a point with nonnegative derivative. This guarantees nonnegative radial parameters when lines are selected through a point.
- **claim-sweeps-cover-tetrahedron** (theorem, [source](#stmt-prop-coverage)): For every $\varepsilon>0$, the compact sets $U_\varepsilon$ and $\mathcal R U_\varepsilon$ cover $K$.
  - Explanation: The upper perturbed sweep and its image under the orthogonal map together cover the tetrahedron for every positive tilt. The cubic cap padding exceeds the interface error.
- **claim-beam-finite-cover** (theorem, [source](#stmt-beam-finite-beams)): For fixed $a\in(0,1/2)$, partition $[0,1]$ into $k$ equal closed intervals and choose a tag $p_I$ in each interval. The two beams admit a covering by $2k$ cylinders with nondegenerate compact triangular perpendicular bases, with axes parallel to $(\beta v(p_I),1)$ and $(-\beta v(p_I),1)$, respectively. Their total normalized cost converges, as $k\to\infty$, to $$\label{beam:C1} C_1=R_a^2\int_0^1 g(v(p))\,dp.$$ In particular, for every $\eta>0$ a finite covering has cost at most $C_1+\eta$.
  - Explanation: The two shortened beams are covered by finitely many cylinders with triangular bases. Their normalized cost converges to an explicit integral as the partition is refined.
- **claim-beam-complement-strip** (theorem, [source](#stmt-beam-coverage)): With $M=25$ in <a href="#beam:width" data-reference-type="eqref" data-reference="beam:width">[beam:width]</a>, one has $K\subset H^-\cup H^+\cup G$ for every $0<a<1/2$.
  - Explanation: What the two beams miss lies inside a tilted band of controlled width, for every admissible shortening parameter.
- **claim-beam-strip-cover** (theorem, [source](#stmt-beam-finite-strip)): Fix $M$ as in Lemma <a href="#beam:coverage" data-reference-type="ref" data-reference="beam:coverage">6</a>. For every fixed $a\in(0,1/2)$ and $\eta>0$, the strip $G$ has a finite covering by cylinders with compact rectangular perpendicular bases and normalized cost at most $C_2+\eta$, where $$\label{beam:C2} C_2=2\sqrt{2}\int_{-1}^{1} \frac{W(t)}{\sqrt{1+(at-a^2t^3)^2}}\,dt.$$
  - Explanation: The residual strip has a finite cover by cylinders with rectangular bases whose normalized cost approaches an explicit integral up to an arbitrary excess.
- **claim-beam-cost-expansion** (bound, [source](#stmt-beam-counterexample)): For $a\in(0,1/2)$, fix $M=25$ in <a href="#beam:width" data-reference-type="eqref" data-reference="beam:width">[beam:width]</a> and put $C(a)=C_1+C_2$, with $C_1,C_2$ as in <a href="#beam:C1" data-reference-type="eqref" data-reference="beam:C1">[beam:C1]</a> and <a href="#beam:C2" data-reference-type="eqref" data-reference="beam:C2">[beam:C2]</a>. For every $\zeta>0$, the tetrahedron $K$ has a finite cylinder cover of normalized cost at most $C(a)+\zeta$, consisting of cylinders with nondegenerate compact triangular perpendicular bases covering $H^-\cup H^+$ and cylinders with horizontal axes and compact rectangular perpendicular bases covering $G$. Moreover, $$\label{beam:strict-gain} C(a)=4-\left(\frac{\sqrt2}{2}-\frac{19}{30}\right)a^2+O(a^3) \qquad(a\downarrow0).$$ In particular, sufficiently small $a>0$ permits a finite cover whose actual total base area is strictly below $A_{\min}(K)/2$.
  - Explanation: The shortened-beam construction has a finite cover whose normalized cost is arbitrarily close to an explicit quantity. The linear terms cancel and the quadratic term is negative, so a small shortening gives a cover below half the minimum projection area.
- **claim-simplex-chord-projection** (theorem, [source](#stmt-prop-simplex)): Let $S\subset\mathbb R^d$, $d\ge2$, be a full-dimensional simplex, and let $u\in\mathbb R^d$ be a unit vector. Write $V$ for its $d$-dimensional volume, $A(u)$ for the $(d-1)$-dimensional volume of its orthogonal projection onto $u^\perp$, and $L(u)$ for the greatest length of a chord of $S$ parallel to $u$. Then $$A(u)L(u)=dV,\qquad \min_{\lVert u\rVert=1}A(u)=\frac{dV}{\operatorname{diam}S}.$$
  - Explanation: For a simplex, the product of directional shadow area and longest parallel chord is a constant multiple of the volume. The minimal shadow is that constant divided by the diameter.
- **claim-four-cap-choices** (classification, [source](#stmt-prop-cap-choices)): For the tetrahedron and paired sweeps just described, each of the following caps gives coverage for every sufficiently small $\varepsilon>0$: $$\begin{aligned} R_\varepsilon(r)&=1+\varepsilon^2(H(r)+\beta), &&\beta>0,\tag{L}\\ R_\varepsilon(r)&=1+\varepsilon^2H(r)+c\varepsilon^3, &&c\ge9/2,\tag{C}\\ R_\varepsilon(r)&=1+\varepsilon^2H(r)+c\varepsilon^{5/2}, &&c>0,\tag{F}\\ R_\varepsilon(r)&=\sqrt{1+2\varepsilon^2(H(r)+\beta)},&&\beta>0.\tag{S} \end{aligned}$$ For (C), coverage in fact holds for every $\varepsilon>0$. Their normalized costs have the respective expansions $$\begin{aligned} Q_R(\varepsilon)&=1+(2\beta-1/30)\varepsilon^2+O(\varepsilon^4), &&\text{(L), (S)}, \label{eq:fixed-cap-cost}\\ Q_R(\varepsilon)&=1-\varepsilon^2/30+2c\varepsilon^3+O(\varepsilon^4), &&\text{(C)}, \label{eq:cubic-cap-cost}\\ Q_R(\varepsilon)&=1-\varepsilon^2/30+2c\varepsilon^{5/2}+O(\varepsilon^4), &&\text{(F)}. \label{eq:fractional-cap-cost} \end{aligned}$$ Thus (L) and (S) give strict saving when $0<\beta<1/60$; (C) and (F) give strict saving for every stated fixed $c$. In each case one first fixes a tilt giving coverage and strict saving, and then chooses a finite sufficiently fine partition.
  - Explanation: Four families of radial caps (linear, cubic, fractional, square root) all give coverage for small tilt. Each has an explicit cost expansion with a strict saving in the stated parameter ranges.

      "conclusion": {
        "anchor": "stmt-thm-radial",
        "exact_quote": "For the data in <a href=\"#eq:radial-sweep\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"eq:radial-sweep\">[eq:radial-sweep]</a>, suppose that $$w'(t)=\\lambda(t)(a+tb)\\qquad(t\\in J)$$ for a continuous real function $\\lambda$. For any finite partition $\\alpha=t_0<t_1<\\cdots<t_N=\\beta$, put $I_i=[t_{i-1},t_i]$ and choose arbitrary tags $c_i\\in I_i$. There are $N$ cylinders covering $\\mathcal S$, with axes parallel to $e+w(c_i)$ and nondegenerate compact triangular perpendicular bases. These covers can be chosen so that their total base area tends to $\\mathcal A$ as the mesh $\\delta=\\max_i|I_i|$ tends to zero, uniformly over the partitions and tags.*\n\n*Alternatively, take each base to be the perpendicular projection of the actual compact sweep with $t\\in I_i$, in the same frozen direction $e+w(c_i)$. These $N$ cylinders cover $\\mathcal S$, and their total base area also tends to $\\mathcal A$, uniformly over the partitions and tags.",
        "source_id": "PM-100.4"
      },

        "anchor": "stmt-cor-tetrahedron",
        "exact_quote": "Every regular tetrahedron $K\\subset\\mathbb R^3$ of positive edge length has a finite cylinder cover $B_i+\\mathbb Ru_i$, with unit axes $u_i$ and nondegenerate compact triangular bases $B_i\\subset u_i^\\perp$, such that $$\\sum_i\\operatorname{area}(B_i)<\\frac{A_{\\min}(K)}2,\n \\qquad\n \\sum_i\\frac{\\operatorname{area}(B_i)}{\\operatorname{area}(\\pi_{u_i^\\perp}K)}<\\frac12.$$",

        "exact_quote": "For fixed $a\\in(0,1/2)$, partition $[0,1]$ into $k$ equal closed intervals and choose a tag $p_I$ in each interval.       "conclusion": {
        "anchor": "stmt-beam-counterexample",
        "exact_quote": "For $a\\in(0,1/2)$, fix $M=25$ in <a href=\"#beam:width\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"beam:width\">[beam:width]</a> and put $C(a)=C_1+C_2$, with $C_1,C_2$ as in <a href=\"#beam:C1\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"beam:C1\">[beam:C1]</a> and <a href=\"#beam:C2\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"beam:C2\">[beam:C2]</a>. For every $\\zeta>0$, the tetrahedron $K$ has a finite cylinder cover of normalized cost at most $C(a)+\\zeta$, consisting of cylinders with nondegenerate compact triangular perpendicular bases covering $H^-\\cup H^+$ and cylinders with horizontal axes and compact rectangular perpendicular bases covering $G$. Moreover, $$\\label{beam:strict-gain}\n C(a)=4-\\left(\\frac{\\sqrt2}{2}-\\frac{19}{30}\\right)a^2+O(a^3)\n \\qquad(a\\downarrow0).$$ In particular, sufficiently small $a>0$ permits a finite cover whose actual total base area is strictly below $A_{\\min}(K)/2$.",
        "source_id": "PM-100.4"
      },
      "evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "stmt-beam-counterexample",
          "exact_quote": "For $a\\in(0,1/2)$, fix $M=25$ in <a href=\"#beam:width\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"beam:width\">[beam:width]</a> and put $C(a)=C_1+C_2$, with $C_1,C_2$ as in <a href=\"#beam:C1\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"beam:C1\">[beam:C1]</a> and <a href=\"#beam:C2\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"beam:C2\">[beam:C2]</a>. For every $\\zeta>0$, the tetrahedron $K$ has a finite cylinder cover of normalized cost at most $C(a)+\\zeta$, consisting of cylinders with nondegenerate compact triangular perpendicular bases covering $H^-\\cup H^+$ and cylinders with horizontal axes and compact rectangular perpendicular bases covering $G$. Moreover, $$\\label{beam:strict-gain}\n C(a)=4-\\left(\\frac{\\sqrt2}{2}-\\frac{19}{30}\\right)a^2+O(a^3)\n \\qquad(a\\downarrow0).$$ In particular, sufficiently small $a>0$ permits a finite cover whose actual total base area is strictly below $A_{\\min}(K)/2$.",
          "source_id": "PM-100.4"
        }
      ],
      "exact_statement": "For $a\\in(0,1/2)$, fix $M=25$ in <a href=\"#beam:width\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"beam:width\">[beam:width]</a> and put $C(a)=C_1+C_2$, with $C_1,C_2$ as in <a href=\"#beam:C1\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"beam:C1\">[beam:C1]</a> and <a href=\"#beam:C2\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"beam:C2\">[beam:C2]</a>.       "conclusion": {
        "anchor": "stmt-prop-simplex",
        "exact_quote": "Let $S\\subset\\mathbb R^d$, $d\\ge2$, be a full-dimensional simplex, and let $u\\in\\mathbb R^d$ be a unit vector. Write $V$ for its $d$-dimensional volume, $A(u)$ for the $(d-1)$-dimensional volume of its orthogonal projection onto $u^\\perp$, and $L(u)$ for the greatest length of a chord of $S$ parallel to $u$. Then $$A(u)L(u)=dV,\\qquad\n \\min_{\\lVert u\\rVert=1}A(u)=\\frac{dV}{\\operatorname{diam}S}.$$",
        "source_id": "PM-100.4"
      },
      "evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "stmt-prop-simplex",
          "exact_quote": "Let $S\\subset\\mathbb R^d$, $d\\ge2$, be a full-dimensional simplex, and let $u\\in\\mathbb R^d$ be a unit vector. Write $V$ for its $d$-dimensional volume, $A(u)$ for the $(d-1)$-dimensional volume of its orthogonal projection onto $u^\\perp$, and $L(u)$ for the greatest length of a chord of $S$ parallel to $u$. Then $$A(u)L(u)=dV,\\qquad\n \\min_{\\lVert u\\rVert=1}A(u)=\\frac{dV}{\\operatorname{diam}S}.$$",
          "source_id": "PM-100.4"
        }
      ],
      "exact_statement": "Let $S\\subset\\mathbb R^d$, $d\\ge2$, be a full-dimensional simplex, and let $u\\in\\mathbb R^d$ be a unit vector. Then $$A(u)L(u)=dV,\\qquad\n \\min_{\\lVert u\\rVert=1}A(u)=\\frac{dV}{\\operatorname{diam}S}.$$",

    {
      "conclusion": {
        "anchor": "stmt-prop-cap-choices",
        "exact_quote": "For the tetrahedron and paired sweeps just described, each of the following caps gives coverage for every sufficiently small $\\varepsilon>0$: $$\\begin{aligned}\n R_\\varepsilon(r)&=1+\\varepsilon^2(H(r)+\\beta), &&\\beta>0,\\tag{L}\\\\\n R_\\varepsilon(r)&=1+\\varepsilon^2H(r)+c\\varepsilon^3, &&c\\ge9/2,\\tag{C}\\\\\n R_\\varepsilon(r)&=1+\\varepsilon^2H(r)+c\\varepsilon^{5/2}, &&c>0,\\tag{F}\\\\\n R_\\varepsilon(r)&=\\sqrt{1+2\\varepsilon^2(H(r)+\\beta)},&&\\beta>0.\\tag{S}\n\\end{aligned}$$ For (C), coverage in fact holds for every $\\varepsilon>0$. Their normalized costs have the respective expansions $$\\begin{aligned}\n Q_R(\\varepsilon)&=1+(2\\beta-1/30)\\varepsilon^2+O(\\varepsilon^4), &&\\text{(L), (S)},\n \\label{eq:fixed-cap-cost}\\\\\n Q_R(\\varepsilon)&=1-\\varepsilon^2/30+2c\\varepsilon^3+O(\\varepsilon^4), &&\\text{(C)},\n \\label{eq:cubic-cap-cost}\\\\\n Q_R(\\varepsilon)&=1-\\varepsilon^2/30+2c\\varepsilon^{5/2}+O(\\varepsilon^4), &&\\text{(F)}.\n \\label{eq:fractional-cap-cost}\n\\end{aligned}$$ Thus (L) and (S) give strict saving when $0<\\beta<1/60$; (C) and (F) give strict saving for every stated fixed $c$. In each case one first fixes a tilt giving coverage and strict saving, and then chooses a finite sufficiently fine partition.",
        "source_id": "PM-100.4"
      },
      "evidence": [
        {

<a id="pm-s2"></a>
## 2. Definitions, validity and bounds

- **cylinder** ([source](#sec:introduction)): A cylinder in $\mathbb R^3$ is a set $B+\mathbb Ru$, where $u$ is a unit vector and $B\subset u^\perp$ is a measurable planar set of finite area.
- **cylinder cost** ([source](#sec:introduction)): Its cost is $\operatorname{area}(B)$.
- **minimum projection area** ([source](#sec:introduction)): For a convex body $K$, write $$A_{\min}(K)=\min_{\lVert u\rVert=1}\operatorname{area}(\pi_{u^\perp}K).$$
- **directionwise normalized cost** ([source](#sec:introduction)): A second covering quantity is the directionwise normalized cost $$\sum_i\frac{\operatorname{area}(B_i)}{\operatorname{area}(\pi_{u_i^\perp}K)},$$ which divides each base area by the shadow in its own axis direction.
- **alignment constants** ([source](#sec:freezing)): Set $$M=\max_J\|\lambda\|,\qquad A=LM,\qquad B=LM/2.$$
- **mesh** ([source](#sec:freezing)): Write $\delta=\max_I d_I$ for the mesh.

| q-complementary-bound | missed intercepts under joint cap failure | ≤ | $16\varepsilon^2$ | Small tilt. |

<a id="pm-s3"></a>
## 3. Proof roadmap

- **step-crossing** [claim](#stmt-lem-crossing) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-crossing): Start below the level and take the first later point where the function equals it. -> crossing point with nonnegative derivative
- **step-radial-triangles** [claim](#stmt-thm-radial) / [proof](#sec:freezing): Bound transverse intercept error quadratically and enclose all intercepts in a slightly extended triangle on each interval. -> covering triangular bases and their exact areas
- **step-radial-limit** [claim](#stmt-thm-radial) / [proof](#sec:freezing): Absorb the collar into a vanishing total error and pass to a Riemann sum by uniform continuity. -> limit equal to the weighted area
- **step-radial-actual** [claim](#stmt-thm-radial) / [proof](#sec:freezing): Squeeze the actual projected sweep between the projected radial sector and the triangle. -> same limit for actual bases
- **step-sweeps-cover** [claim](#stmt-prop-coverage) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-coverage): Select roots with the crossing lemma, then show joint cap failure is contradictory by an exact identity and the cubic padding. -> coverage of the tetrahedron by the two sweeps
- **step-tetrahedron-cost** [claim](#stmt-cor-tetrahedron) / [proof](#sec:tetrahedron): Expand the weighted cost to show a quadratic saving, fix a small tilt, refine the partition, and transport by similarity. -> explicit triangular-base cover below half the minimum projection area
- **step-beam-finite** [claim](#stmt-beam-finite-beams) / [proof](#proof-stmt-beam-finite-beams): Check the hypotheses of the radial theorem in the physical horizontal plane. -> triangular cylinders for both beams with limiting cost
- **step-beam-coverage** [claim](#stmt-beam-coverage) / [proof](#proof-stmt-beam-coverage): Solve for beam parameters of a point outside both beams, derive exact gap identities and uniform estimates, and place the point in the band. -> containment in the union of beams and band
- **step-beam-strip** [claim](#stmt-beam-finite-strip) / [proof](#proof-stmt-beam-finite-strip): Freeze the strip direction on each interval and take rectangular bases with a padded half-width. -> rectangular-base cover with limiting cost
- **step-beam-cost** [claim](#stmt-beam-counterexample) / [proof](#proof-stmt-beam-counterexample): Combine the beam and strip covers, expand both limiting costs in the shortening parameter, and use the strict quadratic saving. -> finite cover with normalized cost below the threshold
- **step-simplex** [claim](#stmt-prop-simplex) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-simplex): Use barycentric coordinates to describe chords, then integrate fiber lengths by a layer-cake argument. -> chord-projection identity and minimal shadow formula
- **step-cap-choices** [claim](#stmt-prop-cap-choices) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-cap-choices): Reuse the root selection and show a padding criterion on the cap corrections, then expand each cost. -> coverage and cost expansions for four cap families

<a id="pm-s4"></a>
## 4. Engineering Triptych

### Panel 1 - epistemic contract

- **inv-cap-quadratic-coefficient**: For the paired tetrahedron sweeps with a linear cap R_eps = 1 + eps^2 (H + beta), beta > 0, the normalized cost has quadratic coefficient 2*beta minus 1/30, which is the sum of the integral of H and 2*beta, minus the integral of D_0/4.
- **inv-strict-saving-threshold**: The linear cap and the square-root cap give a strict saving exactly when the quadratic coefficient 2*beta minus 1/30 is negative, that is when beta is below 1/60.

### Panel 2 - negative knowledge

- **anti-threshold-ignored**: Use a linear or square-root cap with any positive margin beta and expect a cost below half the minimum projection area. -> For beta at or above the threshold the quadratic coefficient is not negative, so no strict saving follows from the expansion.
- **anti-limit-equals-finite**: Treat equality of limiting total areas as equality of the finite bases or of their finite areas at a given mesh. -> Different finite enclosures and the actual swept projections share only the limit; their finite areas and shapes differ.

### Panel 3 - reference implementation and oracle

Applicability: `constructive`. The quadratic cost coefficients of the radial cap table are finite exact rational computations: the cap polynomials are explicit and their integrals over the angular interval are computed exactly. Only this finite exact calculation is covered; the coverage and limit statements are not.

```python
# INPUTS: two integers n and d on stdin (beta = n/d), d > 0.
# PRECONDITIONS: d is a positive integer; n is a nonnegative integer.
# OUTPUTS: the exact rational coefficient of the quadratic term of the cost expansion, as a reduced fraction.
# TIME: constant number of exact rational operations.
# SPACE: constant.
# ARITHMETIC: exact rational arithmetic with Python fractions, no floating point.
# FAILURE: malformed input raises an exception and a nonzero exit status.
import sys
from fractions import Fraction

def integ(poly):
    return sum(c * (Fraction(2, p + 1) if p % 2 == 0 else 0) for p, c in poly.items())

n, d = map(int, sys.stdin.read().split())
beta = Fraction(n, d)
H = {2: Fraction(1, 2), 4: Fraction(1, 2)}
D0 = {0: Fraction(1, 4), 2: Fraction(5, 2), 4: Fraction(1, 4)}
cost = integ(H) + 2*beta - integ(D0)/4
print(cost)
```

## 5. Domain engineering contracts

| domain | applies | strength | maturity | basis | why |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| software | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The paper proves existence and convergence of geometric covers. It specifies no program, library or interface, so there is no software artefact to build directly from the statements. |
| games_simulation | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | Cylinder covers of a tetrahedron are measured by total perpendicular base area. The paper says nothing about collision, rendering or real-time simulation, so any such use would be an unsupported transfer. |
| tools | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | No instrument or checker is proposed. The explicit triangle formulas serve the proofs and are not offered as a tool. |
| algorithms | True | moderate | adaptable | inferred | For a given tagged partition, the proof of the radial theorem builds one enclosing triangle per interval from explicit formulas. This is a finite recipe, given evaluations of the cap R, the direction map w and the bound M on lambda. |
| data_structures | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The results concern areas and coverage in space. No storage, indexing or query structure is defined, and the cover is just a list of cylinders. |
| hardware_accelerators | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | No arithmetic circuit, parallel scheme or hardware cost model is discussed. The per-interval triangles are independent, but the paper does not use that for acceleration. |
| crypto_security | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | Nothing in the paper concerns hardness, secrecy or adversaries. Cylinder cover geometry gives no cryptographic primitive. |
| machine_learning | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The paper has no data, learning objective or statistical estimation. Its tagged Riemann-sum convergence is a deterministic geometric fact. |
| optimisation_operations_research | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The paper lowers a cover cost by explicit construction and does not determine the infimum cost or give an optimisation procedure. The half-area question stays negatively answered only for tetrahedra. |
| distributed_systems | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | There are no processes, messages or fault models. The partition into intervals is a mathematical device, not a distribution of work. |
| other | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The main contribution is pure convex and discrete geometry about covering a tetrahedron by cylinders. It has no further engineering use beyond the algorithmic reading above. |

<a id="pm-s6"></a>
## 6. Applicability limits and search

- **limit-radial-alignment** (hypothesis): The radial cover theorem needs the direction field to satisfy the alignment condition, with the derivative of w proportional to a+tb through a continuous function lambda. Without it the frozen-direction loss need not vanish. Blocked use: ['Applying the triangular cover and its area limit to arbitrary direction fields', 'Treating the paper as an approximation theorem for general velocity fields']
- **limit-compact-sweep-data** (hypothesis): The sweep is a compact set with a bounded segment half-length, a nonnegative continuous radial cap and a compact parameter interval. Nothing is claimed for unbounded sweeps. Blocked use: ['Covering unbounded cones or infinite segment sweeps by finitely many cylinders with convergent cost']
- **limit-limit-not-finite** (edge_case): The convergence of total base area holds only as the mesh tends to zero. Triangular enclosures, polygonal feet and actual projected sweeps share the limit but have different finite areas. Blocked use: ['Substituting triangle areas for actual projected areas at a fixed coarse partition', 'Reading the equal limit as equal finite costs']
- **limit-no-convergence-rate** (formal_scope): No convergence rate for the whole sum is established for an arbitrary continuous cap. Only the limit is proved, uniformly over partitions and tags. Blocked use: ['Choosing a partition size from an assumed error rate without a separate estimate']
- **limit-tetrahedron-only** (counterexample_scope): The sub-half-area covers are constructed for regular tetrahedra, with transport by similarity. The corollary says nothing about other convex bodies, and for ellipsoids a stronger lower bound is cited. Blocked use: ['Claiming a cost below half the minimum projection for ellipsoids or arbitrary convex bodies']
- **limit-parameter-order** (hypothesis): The strict saving needs the parameters chosen in order: first the tilt or shortening parameter small enough for coverage and strict saving, then the finite partition fine enough to use only part of the saving. A partition fixed first need not give a saving. Blocked use: ['Fixing the partition before the tilt and expecting a cost below half the minimum projection area']
- **limit-cap-margin-threshold** (edge_case): For the linear and square-root caps a strict saving needs the margin to lie strictly between zero and one sixtieth. A larger margin makes the quadratic coefficient nonnegative. Blocked use: ['Using a linear or square-root cap with a large positive margin and expecting a saving']
- **limit-asymptotic-regime** (approximation): The cost expansions in the tilt or shortening parameter are asymptotic with big-O remainders. The text gives no explicit threshold for the small parameter. Blocked use: ['Using a numerical value of the tilt as certified to give a saving', 'Quoting an explicit smallness bound for the tilt']
- **limit-nonconstructive-constants** (unknown): The existence of a small tilt and a fine partition is shown by limits, and no explicit tilt or cylinder count is given. The count is not asserted to stay bounded as the tilt tends to zero. Blocked use: ['Reading the result as a bound on the number of cylinders needed']

<a id="pm-formal"></a>
### Formal statement (Lean 4)

Corpus status: formalized in the source corpus (toolchain `leanprover/lean4:v4.34.1`, Mathlib `d13f23b723b8a846827a245b89c10fc7d3f11612`). Not re-checked here; the statements give the exact quantifiers and hypotheses, not a proof.

Scope note of family 100 (`lean/docs/100.md`, verbatim):

> The following describes the scope of the Lean formalization related to the following accompanying paper(s):
>
> - [Finite angular cylinder covers below the half-area bound](../../preprints/Finite-angular-cylinder-covers-below-the-half-area-bound-September-27-2026/main.pdf)
> - [Finite cylinder approximation of ruled sets](../../preprints/Finite-cylinder-approximation-of-ruled-sets-September-27-2026/main.pdf)
> - [Slope-field perturbations of the two-cylinder covering](../../preprints/Slope-field-perturbations-of-the-two-cylinder-covering-September-27-2026/main.pdf)
> - [Finite triangular approximation of radial sweeps](../../preprints/Finite-triangular-approximation-of-radial-sweeps-September-27-2026/main.pdf)
>
> ## Scope
>
> The half-area cylinder-covering conjecture predicts that a cylinder cover of a convex body has total perpendicular-base area at least half its smallest projection area. The formalization constructs finite covers of a regular tetrahedron by cylinders with compact triangular bases whose total area is strictly below that bound. For the explicit small parameter $\varepsilon$, the normalized area is $1/2-(13/6000)\varepsilon^2+O(\varepsilon^4)$, giving the strict saving.
>
> It also gives a counterexample to the directionwise normalized half-bound. The broader Comparator file includes the companion ruled-set and radial-sweep approximation results.
>
> The formalization approximates compact ruled families of line segments by finite cylinder covers with perpendicular-base area at most the integral projection cost plus any prescribed positive error. The selected results cover the stated $C^1$ hyperbolic differential condition, square-zero and nilpotent cases, physical rescalings, logarithmic and product-cell estimates, and singular cases. Compact label sets need not have regular boundary.
>
> The differential and segment-length hypotheses remain part of each result. The aggregate Comparator file also contains the companion tetrahedron counterexamples and radial-sweep estimates.
>
> The formalization proves that every regular tetrahedron has a finite cylinder cover whose total perpendicular-base area is strictly less than half its minimum projection area. It supplies covers with parallelogram bases and also proves the directionwise normalized cost is below one half. These are counterexamples to the two cylinder-covering bounds studied in the paper.
>
> The linked aggregate includes the accompanying explicit angular covers and finite approximation theorems for ruled sets and radial sweeps. The affine extension of the directionwise conclusion to every nondegenerate tetrahedron is outside the selected regular-tetrahedron statements.
>
> The formalization proves finite triangular-cylinder approximation for radially aligned segment sweeps. For every tagged partition of the parameter interval, it constructs one cylinder per interval with the prescribed radial direction, and the total perpendicular-base area converges to the weighted parameter area as the mesh tends to zero. It also covers the corresponding actual swept set under the stated geometric hypotheses.
>
> The same aggregate includes finite cylinder covers of regular tetrahedra below half the minimum projection area. These are the radial approximation and covering consequences relevant to the paper.

Comparator source `CylinderCovering.lean` (19340 bytes, sha256 `b5322a9fcf803a0d62ebf05baf60bf38097f9fcdbcca7bf884d8d4eb940b3606`), verbatim:

```lean
import Mathlib

namespace OAI

section
open Set MeasureTheory
open scoped InnerProductSpace BigOperators Interval

noncomputable section
namespace TriangularCovering

abbrev Space := EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)

def H : ℝ := Real.sqrt 2

def point (x y t : ℝ) : Space := !₂[x, y, H * t]

def K : Set Space :=
  {z | ∃ x y t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t ∧ t ≤ 1 ∧ |x| ≤ 1 - t ∧ |y| ≤ t ∧ z = point x y t}

def projection (u z : Space) : Space := z - (inner ℝ u z) • u

def area (B : Set Space) : ℝ := (Measure.euclideanHausdorffMeasure 2 B).toReal

def shadowArea (u : Space) : ℝ := area (projection u '' K)

def A_min : ℝ := sInf {a : ℝ | ∃ u : Space, ‖u‖ = 1 ∧ a = shadowArea u}

structure TriCylinder where
  axis : Space
  base : Set Space
  unit_axis : ‖axis‖ = 1
  perpendicular : ∀ z ∈ base, inner ℝ axis z = 0
  triangular : ∃ v : Fin 3 → Space, AffineIndependent ℝ v ∧
    base = convexHull ℝ (Set.range v)
  compact : IsCompact base
  convex : Convex ℝ base
  measurable : MeasurableSet base
  finite_area : Measure.euclideanHausdorffMeasure 2 base ≠ ⊤

def TriCylinder.carrier (c : TriCylinder) : Set Space :=
  {z | ∃ b ∈ c.base, ∃ r : ℝ, z = b + r • c.axis}

def Covers {m : ℕ} (c : Fin m → TriCylinder) : Prop :=
  ∀ z ∈ K, ∃ i : Fin m, z ∈ (c i).carrier

def totalArea {m : ℕ} (c : Fin m → TriCylinder) : ℝ :=
  ∑ i : Fin m, area (c i).base

def relativeCost {m : ℕ} (c : Fin m → TriCylinder) : ℝ :=
  ∑ i : Fin m, area (c i).base / shadowArea (c i).axis

def n (ε : ℝ) : ℕ := ⌈2 / ε ^ 2⌉₊
end TriangularCovering
end
end

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

section
open Set MeasureTheory
open scoped InnerProductSpace BigOperators Interval

noncomputable section
namespace TriangularCovering

def η : ℝ := 1 / 1000

def alpha (a b : ℝ) : ℝ := (1 + a * b) / 4

def beta (a b : ℝ) : ℝ := (a + b) / 4

def d (q : ℝ) : ℝ := q ^ 2 * (1 + q ^ 2) / 16

def cutoffCoeff (a b : ℝ) : ℝ := η + max (d a) (d b)

def step (N : ℕ) : ℝ := 2 / N

def mesh (N i : ℕ) : ℝ := -1 + i * step N

def cutoff (ε a b : ℝ) : ℝ := 1 / 2 + ε ^ 2 * cutoffCoeff a b

def speedSq (a b : ℝ) : ℝ := (alpha a b)^2 + 2*(beta a b)^2

def normalizedTerm (ε a b : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  (b-a) * (cutoff ε a b)^2 / Real.sqrt (1+ε^2*speedSq a b)

end TriangularCovering
end
end

section
open Set MeasureTheory
open scoped InnerProductSpace BigOperators Interval

noncomputable section
namespace TriangularCovering

def tiltV (ε A B : ℝ) : Space := !₂[1, ε*A, H*ε*B]

def tiltW (ε A B : ℝ) : Space := !₂[-ε*A, 1, H*ε*B]

def lowerVertices (a b T : ℝ) : Fin 3 → Space :=
  ![point 0 0 0, point 0 (a*T) T, point 0 (b*T) T]

def upperVertices (a b T : ℝ) : Fin 3 → Space :=
  ![point 0 0 1, point (a*T) 0 (1-T), point (b*T) 0 (1-T)]

end TriangularCovering
end
end

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

section
open Set MeasureTheory
open scoped InnerProductSpace BigOperators Interval

noncomputable section
namespace TriangularCovering.Planar

abbrev Coord := Fin 2 → ℝ

def standard : Set Coord := {x | 0 ≤ x 0 ∧ 0 ≤ x 1 ∧ x 0 + x 1 ≤ 1}

def vertices : Fin 3 → Coord := ![0, ![1, 0], ![0, 1] ]

end TriangularCovering.Planar

namespace TriangularCovering

section Triangles
variable {E F : Type*} [AddCommGroup E] [Module ℝ E] [AddCommGroup F] [Module ℝ F]

def triangle (a b c : E) : Set E := convexHull ℝ (range (![a, b, c] : Fin 3 → E))

end Triangles
end TriangularCovering
end
end

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

section
open Set MeasureTheory
open scoped InnerProductSpace

noncomputable section
namespace TriangularCovering

def lowerAngularIndex {N : ℕ} (j : Fin N) : Fin (2*N) :=
  Fin.cast (two_mul N).symm (Fin.castAdd N j)

def upperAngularIndex {N : ℕ} (j : Fin N) : Fin (2*N) :=
  Fin.cast (two_mul N).symm (Fin.natAdd N j)

def lowerAngularAxis (ε : ℝ) (j : ℕ) : Space :=
  let a := mesh (n ε) j
  let b := mesh (n ε) (j+1)
  unit (tiltV ε (alpha a b) (beta a b))

def upperAngularAxis (ε : ℝ) (j : ℕ) : Space :=
  let a := mesh (n ε) j
  let b := mesh (n ε) (j+1)
  unit (tiltW ε (alpha a b) (beta a b))

def lowerAngularBase (ε : ℝ) (j : ℕ) : Set Space :=
  let a := mesh (n ε) j
  let b := mesh (n ε) (j+1)
  let v := lowerVertices a b (cutoff ε a b)
  projection (lowerAngularAxis ε j) '' triangle (v 0) (v 1) (v 2)

def upperAngularBase (ε : ℝ) (j : ℕ) : Set Space :=
  let a := mesh (n ε) j
  let b := mesh (n ε) (j+1)
  let v := upperVertices a b (cutoff ε a b)
  projection (upperAngularAxis ε j) '' triangle (v 0) (v 1) (v 2)

def ExplicitAngularFamily (ε : ℝ) (c : Fin (2*n ε) → TriCylinder) : Prop :=
  ∀ j : Fin (n ε),
    (c (lowerAngularIndex j)).axis = lowerAngularAxis ε j ∧
    (c (lowerAngularIndex j)).base = lowerAngularBase ε j ∧
    (c (upperAngularIndex j)).axis = upperAngularAxis ε j ∧
    (c (upperAngularIndex j)).base = upperAngularBase ε j

def AngularAreaStatement : Prop :=
  ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε ≤ 1 →
    ∃ c : Fin (2*n ε) → TriCylinder,
      ExplicitAngularFamily ε c ∧
      totalArea c/H = ∑ j : Fin (n ε),
        normalizedTerm ε (mesh (n ε) j) (mesh (n ε) (j+1)) ∧
      |totalArea c/H - (1/2-(13/6000:ℝ)*ε^2)| ≤ 2*ε^4

def ExplicitAngularStatement : Prop :=
  A_min = H ∧
  ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε ≤ 1/2000 →
    ∃ c : Fin (2*n ε) → TriCylinder,
      ExplicitAngularFamily ε c ∧ Covers c ∧
      |totalArea c/H - (1/2-(13/6000:ℝ)*ε^2)| ≤ 2*ε^4 ∧
      totalArea c < A_min/2 ∧ relativeCost c < 1/2

end TriangularCovering
end
end

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

section
noncomputable section
open Set MeasureTheory Filter Metric
open scoped Topology ContDiff Pointwise
namespace RuledApproximation

abbrev Plane := EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)
abbrev Space := EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)
def vec2 (x y : ℝ) : Plane := WithLp.toLp 2 ![x, y]
def vec3 (x y z : ℝ) : Space := WithLp.toLp 2 ![x, y, z]
def axial (s : ℝ) (p : Plane) : Space := vec3 s (p 0) (p 1)

def IsParallelogram {E : Type*} [AddCommGroup E] [Module ℝ E] (P : Set E) : Prop :=
  ∃ c u v : E, LinearIndependent ℝ ![u, v] ∧
    P = {x | ∃ s ∈ Icc (0 : ℝ) 1, ∃ t ∈ Icc (0 : ℝ) 1,
      x = c + s • u + t • v}
structure Cylinder where
  axis : Space
  unit_axis : ‖axis‖ = 1
  base : Set ((Submodule.span ℝ ({axis} : Set Space))ᗮ)

namespace Cylinder

def carrier (C : Cylinder) : Set Space :=
  {x | ∃ b ∈ C.base, ∃ t : ℝ, x = (b : Space) + t • C.axis}

def area (C : Cylinder) : ℝ := volume.real C.base

def parallelogram (C : Cylinder) : Prop := IsParallelogram C.base

def openBounded (C : Cylinder) : Prop :=
  IsOpen C.base ∧ Bornology.IsBounded C.base ∧ MeasurableSet C.base
end Cylinder

def familySet {n : ℕ} (C : Fin n → Cylinder) : Set Space := ⋃ i, (C i).carrier
def familyArea {n : ℕ} (C : Fin n → Cylinder) : ℝ := ∑ i, (C i).area

def HasFiniteCover (shape : Cylinder → Prop) (S : Set Space) (A : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∃ n : ℕ, ∃ C : Fin n → Cylinder,
    (∀ i, shape (C i)) ∧ S ⊆ familySet C ∧ familyArea C ≤ A

def SmoothNear (n : WithTop ℕ∞) (D : Set Plane) (v : Plane → Plane) : Prop :=
  ∃ U : Set Plane, IsOpen U ∧ D ⊆ U ∧ ContDiffOn ℝ n v U

def HyperbolicAt (L : ℝ) (v : Plane → Plane) (p : Plane) : Prop :=
  ∃ lam : ℝ, 0 < L * lam ∧ L * lam < 1 ∧
    Module.End.HasEigenvalue (fderiv ℝ v p).toLinearMap lam ∧
    Module.End.HasEigenvalue (fderiv ℝ v p).toLinearMap (-lam)

def weight (v : Plane) : ℝ := 1 / Real.sqrt (1 + ‖v‖ ^ 2)
def physicalWeight (h : ℝ) (w : Plane) : ℝ :=
  1 / Real.sqrt (1 + w 0 ^ 2 + h ^ 2 * w 1 ^ 2)

def ruled (D : Set Plane) (v : Plane → Plane) (L : ℝ) : Set Space :=
  {z | ∃ p ∈ D, ∃ r : ℝ, |r| ≤ L ∧
    z = vec3 (p 0 + r * v p 0) (p 1 + r * v p 1) r}

def physicalRuled (h : ℝ) (D : Set Plane) (v : Plane → Plane) (L : ℝ) : Set Space :=
  {z | ∃ p ∈ D, ∃ s : ℝ, |s| ≤ L ∧
    z = vec3 s (p 0 + s * v p 0) (h * (p 1 + s * v p 1))}

def HyperbolicStatement : Prop :=
  ∀ (D : Set Plane) (v : Plane → Plane) (L : ℝ),
    IsCompact D → SmoothNear 1 D v → 0 < L →
    (∀ p ∈ D, HyperbolicAt L v p) →
    ∀ e : ℝ, 0 < e → HasFiniteCover Cylinder.parallelogram (ruled D v L)
      ((∫ p in D, weight (v p)) + e)

def unitSquare : Set Plane := {x | ∀ i, |x i| ≤ (1 : ℝ) / 2}

def IsSquareTile (T : Set Plane) : Prop :=
  ∃ c : Plane, ∃ R : Plane ≃ₗᵢ[ℝ] Plane, ∃ a : ℝ, 0 < a ∧
    T = (fun x => c + a • R x) '' unitSquare

def auxiliaryCylinder (T : Set Plane) (g : Plane) : Set Space :=
  {z | ∃ s : ℝ, ∃ b ∈ T, z = axial s (b + s • g)}

def firstPhysicalMap (h : ℝ) (z : Space) : Space := vec3 (z 0) (z 1) (h * z 2)
def secondPhysicalMap (h a : ℝ) (z : Space) : Space :=
  vec3 (z 1) (z 0) (h * (a - z 2))
end RuledApproximation
end
end

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

section
noncomputable section
open Set MeasureTheory
open scoped InnerProductSpace

namespace CylinderCovering

abbrev Space := EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)

def IsRegularTetrahedron (K : Set Space) : Prop :=
  ∃ v : Fin 4 → Space, AffineIndependent ℝ v ∧
    ∃ edge : ℝ, 0 < edge ∧
      (∀ i j : Fin 4, i ≠ j → dist (v i) (v j) = edge) ∧
      K = convexHull ℝ (range v)

def shadowArea (K : Set Space) (u : Space) : ℝ :=
  TriangularCovering.area (TriangularCovering.projection u '' K)

def minimumShadowArea (K : Set Space) : ℝ :=
  sInf {a : ℝ | ∃ u : Space, ‖u‖ = 1 ∧ a = shadowArea K u}

def triangularCovers {n : ℕ} (K : Set Space)
    (C : Fin n → TriangularCovering.TriCylinder) : Prop :=
  ∀ x ∈ K, ∃ i : Fin n, x ∈ (C i).carrier

def triangularRelativeCost {n : ℕ} (K : Set Space)
    (C : Fin n → TriangularCovering.TriCylinder) : ℝ :=
  ∑ i, TriangularCovering.area (C i).base / shadowArea K (C i).axis

def relativeCost {n : ℕ} (K : Set Space)
    (C : Fin n → RuledApproximation.Cylinder) : ℝ :=
  ∑ i, (C i).area / shadowArea K (C i).axis

def AngularExplicitStatement : Prop :=
  TriangularCovering.AngularAreaStatement ∧ TriangularCovering.ExplicitAngularStatement

def RegularTriangularCounterexampleStatement : Prop :=
  ∀ K : Set Space, IsRegularTetrahedron K →
    ∃ n : ℕ, ∃ C : Fin n → TriangularCovering.TriCylinder,
      triangularCovers K C ∧
      TriangularCovering.totalArea C < minimumShadowArea K / 2 ∧
      triangularRelativeCost K C < 1 / 2

def RegularParallelogramCounterexampleStatement : Prop :=
  ∀ K : Set Space, IsRegularTetrahedron K →
    ∃ n : ℕ, ∃ C : Fin n → RuledApproximation.Cylinder,
      (∀ i, (C i).parallelogram) ∧
      K ⊆ RuledApproximation.familySet C ∧
      RuledApproximation.familyArea C < minimumShadowArea K / 2 ∧
      relativeCost K C < 1 / 2

def RegularOpenCounterexampleStatement : Prop :=
  ∀ K : Set Space, IsRegularTetrahedron K →
    ∃ n : ℕ, ∃ C : Fin n → RuledApproximation.Cylinder,
      (∀ i, (C i).openBounded) ∧
      K ⊆ RuledApproximation.familySet C ∧
      RuledApproximation.familyArea C < minimumShadowArea K / 2 ∧
      relativeCost K C < 1 / 2

end CylinderCovering
end
end

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

section
noncomputable section
open Set MeasureTheory Filter
open scoped Topology ContDiff
namespace RuledApproximation

def NilpotentC1Statement : Prop :=
  ∀ (D : Set Plane) (G : Plane → Plane), IsCompact D → SmoothNear 1 D G →
    (∀ b ∈ D, (fderiv ℝ G b).comp (fderiv ℝ G b) = 0) →
    ∀ h eps : ℝ, 0 < h → 0 < eps →
    ∃ n : ℕ, ∃ T : Fin n → Set Plane, ∃ g : Fin n → Plane,
      (∀ i, IsSquareTile (T i)) ∧
      physicalRuled 1 D G 1 ⊆ ⋃ i, auxiliaryCylinder (T i) (g i) ∧
      (∑ i, volume.real (T i) * physicalWeight h (g i)) ≤
        (∫ b in D, physicalWeight h (G b)) + eps ∧
      ∀ a : ℝ, ∃ C₁ C₂ : Fin n → Cylinder,
        (∀ i, (C₁ i).parallelogram ∧ (C₂ i).parallelogram ∧
          (C₁ i).carrier = firstPhysicalMap h '' auxiliaryCylinder (T i) (g i) ∧
          (C₂ i).carrier = secondPhysicalMap h a '' auxiliaryCylinder (T i) (g i) ∧
          (C₁ i).area = h * volume.real (T i) * physicalWeight h (g i) ∧
          (C₂ i).area = h * volume.real (T i) * physicalWeight h (g i))

end RuledApproximation
end
end

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

section
noncomputable section
namespace CylinderCovering.RadialRiemann
open Set

def step (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) : ℝ := (b - a) / N

def point (a b : ℝ) (N i : ℕ) : ℝ := a + i * step a b N

def cell (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) (i : Fin N) : Set ℝ :=
  Icc (point a b N i) (point a b N (i + 1))

def IsTagged (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) (tag : Fin N → ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ i, tag i ∈ cell a b N i

def capMax (R : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) (i : Fin N) : ℝ :=
  sSup (R '' cell a b N i)
end CylinderCovering.RadialRiemann
end
end

run_cmd Lean.modifyEnv fun env => Lean.Meta.auxLemmasExt.setState env {}

section
open Set MeasureTheory Filter
open scoped InnerProductSpace Topology

noncomputable section

namespace CylinderCovering.RadialSweep

abbrev Space := TriangularCovering.Space

structure Data where
  e : Space
  a : Space
  b : Space
  p₀ : Space
  α : ℝ
  β : ℝ
  L : ℝ
  R : ℝ → ℝ
  lam : ℝ → ℝ
  w : ℝ → Space
  interval_nonempty : α < β
  length_pos : 0 < L
  normal_unit : ‖e‖ = 1
  a_perp : inner ℝ e a = 0
  b_perp : inner ℝ e b = 0
  origin_perp : inner ℝ e p₀ = 0
  independent : LinearIndependent ℝ ![a, b]
  cap_continuous : ContinuousOn R (Icc α β)
  cap_nonneg : ∀ t ∈ Icc α β, 0 ≤ R t
  scalar_continuous : ContinuousOn lam (Icc α β)
  velocity_continuous : ContinuousOn w (Icc α β)
  velocity_perp : ∀ t ∈ Icc α β, inner ℝ e (w t) = 0
  aligned : ∀ t ∈ Icc α β,
    HasDerivWithinAt w (lam t • (a + t • b)) (Icc α β) t

def sweep (D : Data) : Set Space :=
  {z | ∃ t ∈ Icc D.α D.β, ∃ ρ ∈ Icc (0 : ℝ) (D.R t),
    ∃ s : ℝ, |s| ≤ D.L ∧
      z = D.p₀ + ρ • (D.a + t • D.b) + s • (D.e + D.w t)}

def kappa (D : Data) : ℝ :=
  Real.sqrt (inner ℝ D.a D.a * inner ℝ D.b D.b - (inner ℝ D.a D.b) ^ 2)

def direction (D : Data) (t : ℝ) : Space :=
  ‖D.e + D.w t‖⁻¹ • (D.e + D.w t)

def weightedArea (D : Data) : ℝ :=
  kappa D * ∫ t in D.α..D.β,
    D.R t ^ 2 / (2 * Real.sqrt (1 + ‖D.w t‖ ^ 2))

def scalarBound (D : Data) : ℝ := sSup ((fun t => |D.lam t|) '' Icc D.α D.β)

def radialError (D : Data) : ℝ := D.L * scalarBound D

def collar (D : Data) : ℝ := 3 * radialError D + 1

def midpoint (D : Data) (N : ℕ) (i : Fin N) : ℝ :=
  (RadialRiemann.point D.α D.β N i + RadialRiemann.point D.α D.β N (i + 1)) / 2

def leftTip (D : Data) (N : ℕ) : ℝ := -collar D * RadialRiemann.step D.α D.β N

def rightEdge (D : Data) (N : ℕ) (i : Fin N) : ℝ :=
  RadialRiemann.capMax D.R D.α D.β N i + radialError D * RadialRiemann.step D.α D.β N

def coordinateMap (D : Data) (p : ℝ × ℝ) : Space := D.p₀ + p.1 • D.a + p.2 • D.b

def frozenVertices (D : Data) (N : ℕ) (i : Fin N) : Fin 3 → Space :=
  let l := leftTip D N
  let r := rightEdge D N i
  let m := midpoint D N i
  let d := RadialRiemann.step D.α D.β N
  ![coordinateMap D (l, m * l),
    coordinateMap D (r, m * r - d / 2 * (r - l)),
    coordinateMap D (r, m * r + d / 2 * (r - l))]

def frozenTriangle (D : Data) (N : ℕ) (i : Fin N) : Set Space :=
  convexHull ℝ (Set.range (frozenVertices D N i))

def frozenBase (D : Data) (N : ℕ) (tag : Fin N → ℝ) (i : Fin N) : Set Space :=
  TriangularCovering.projection (direction D (tag i)) '' frozenTriangle D N i

def frozenArea (D : Data) (N : ℕ) (tag : Fin N → ℝ) (i : Fin N) : ℝ :=
  kappa D * RadialRiemann.step D.α D.β N *
    (RadialRiemann.capMax D.R D.α D.β N i +
      (radialError D + collar D) * RadialRiemann.step D.α D.β N) ^ 2 /
    (2 * Real.sqrt (1 + ‖D.w (tag i)‖ ^ 2))

def localSweep (D : Data) (N : ℕ) (i : Fin N) : Set Space :=
  {z | ∃ t ∈ RadialRiemann.cell D.α D.β N i,
    ∃ ρ ∈ Icc (0 : ℝ) (D.R t), ∃ s : ℝ, |s| ≤ D.L ∧
      z = D.p₀ + ρ • (D.a + t • D.b) + s • (D.e + D.w t)}

def actualBase (D : Data) (N : ℕ) (tag : Fin N → ℝ) (i : Fin N) : Set Space :=
  TriangularCovering.projection (direction D (tag i)) '' localSweep D N i

def cylinder (u : Space) (B : Set Space) : Set Space :=
  {z | ∃ b ∈ B, ∃ s : ℝ, z = b + s • u}

def actualCost (D : Data) (N : ℕ) (tag : Fin N → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  ∑ i : Fin N, TriangularCovering.area (actualBase D N tag i)

def AlignedStatement : Prop :=
  ∀ D : Data, ∀ tag : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ,
    (∀ N, RadialRiemann.IsTagged D.α D.β N (tag N)) →
    ∃ C : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → TriangularCovering.TriCylinder,
      (∀ N, 0 < N →
        (∀ i, (C N i).axis = direction D (tag N i) ∧
          (C N i).base = frozenBase D N (tag N) i ∧
          TriangularCovering.area (C N i).base = frozenArea D N (tag N) i) ∧
        (∀ z ∈ sweep D, ∃ i : Fin N, z ∈ (C N i).carrier)) ∧
      Tendsto (fun N => TriangularCovering.totalArea (C N)) atTop (𝓝 (weightedArea D))

def ActualSweepStatement : Prop :=
  ∀ D : Data, ∀ tag : (N : ℕ) → Fin N → ℝ,
    (∀ N, RadialRiemann.IsTagged D.α D.β N (tag N)) →
    (∀ N, 0 < N →
      (∀ i : Fin N, ‖direction D (tag N i)‖ = 1 ∧
        IsCompact (actualBase D N (tag N) i) ∧
        MeasurableSet (actualBase D N (tag N) i) ∧
        Measure.euclideanHausdorffMeasure 2 (actualBase D N (tag N) i) ≠ ⊤ ∧
        (∀ z ∈ actualBase D N (tag N) i, inner ℝ (direction D (tag N i)) z = 0) ∧
        actualBase D N (tag N) i ⊆ frozenBase D N (tag N) i) ∧
      (∀ z ∈ sweep D, ∃ i : Fin N,
        z ∈ cylinder (direction D (tag N i)) (actualBase D N (tag N) i))) ∧
    (∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ∀ᶠ N in atTop, actualCost D N (tag N) ≤ weightedArea D + ε) ∧
    Filter.limsup (fun N => actualCost D N (tag N)) atTop ≤ weightedArea D

end CylinderCovering.RadialSweep
end
end

namespace CylinderCovering

theorem fourPaperMain :
    AngularExplicitStatement ∧
    RuledApproximation.HyperbolicStatement ∧
    RuledApproximation.NilpotentC1Statement ∧
    RegularParallelogramCounterexampleStatement ∧
    RegularOpenCounterexampleStatement ∧
    RadialSweep.AlignedStatement ∧
    RadialSweep.ActualSweepStatement := by
  sorry

end CylinderCovering

end OAI
```

      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "structure",
      "name": "TriCylinder",
      "statement_lean": "structure TriCylinder where\n  axis : Space\n  base : Set Space\n  unit_axis : ‖axis‖ = 1\n  perpendicular : ∀ z ∈ base, inner ℝ axis z = 0\n  triangular : ∃ v : Fin 3 → Space, AffineIndependent ℝ v ∧\n    base = convexHull ℝ (Set.range v)\n  compact : IsCompact base\n  convex : Convex ℝ base\n  measurable : MeasurableSet base\n  finite_area : Measure.euclideanHausdorffMeasure 2 base ≠ ⊤"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "TriCylinder.carrier",
      "statement_lean": "def TriCylinder.carrier (c : TriCylinder) : Set Space :=\n  {z | ∃ b ∈ c.base, ∃ r : ℝ, z = b + r • c.axis}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "Covers",
      "statement_lean": "def Covers {m : ℕ} (c : Fin m → TriCylinder) : Prop :=\n  ∀ z ∈ K, ∃ i : Fin m, z ∈ (c i).carrier"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "totalArea",
      "statement_lean": "def totalArea {m : ℕ} (c : Fin m → TriCylinder) : ℝ :=\n  ∑ i : Fin m, area (c i).base"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "relativeCost",
      "statement_lean": "def relativeCost {m : ℕ} (c : Fin m → TriCylinder) : ℝ :=\n  ∑ i : Fin m, area (c i).base / shadowArea (c i).axis"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "n",
      "statement_lean": "def n (ε : ℝ) : ℕ := ⌈2 / ε ^ 2⌉₊"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "η",
      "statement_lean": "def η : ℝ := 1 / 1000"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "alpha",
      "statement_lean": "def alpha (a b : ℝ) : ℝ := (1 + a * b) / 4"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "beta",
      "statement_lean": "def beta (a b : ℝ) : ℝ := (a + b) / 4"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "d",
      "statement_lean": "def d (q : ℝ) : ℝ := q ^ 2 * (1 + q ^ 2) / 16"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "cutoffCoeff",
      "statement_lean": "def cutoffCoeff (a b : ℝ) : ℝ := η + max (d a) (d b)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "step",
      "statement_lean": "def step (N : ℕ) : ℝ := 2 / N"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "mesh",
      "statement_lean": "def mesh (N i : ℕ) : ℝ := -1 + i * step N"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "cutoff",
      "statement_lean": "def cutoff (ε a b : ℝ) : ℝ := 1 / 2 + ε ^ 2 * cutoffCoeff a b"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "speedSq",
      "statement_lean": "def speedSq (a b : ℝ) : ℝ := (alpha a b)^2 + 2*(beta a b)^2"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "normalizedTerm",
      "statement_lean": "def normalizedTerm (ε a b : ℝ) : ℝ :=\n  (b-a) * (cutoff ε a b)^2 / Real.sqrt (1+ε^2*speedSq a b)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "unit",
      "statement_lean": "def unit (v : Space) : Space := ‖v‖⁻¹ • v"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "tiltV",
      "statement_lean": "def tiltV (ε A B : ℝ) : Space := !₂[1, ε*A, H*ε*B]"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "tiltW",
      "statement_lean": "def tiltW (ε A B : ℝ) : Space := !₂[-ε*A, 1, H*ε*B]"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "lowerVertices",
      "statement_lean": "def lowerVertices (a b T : ℝ) : Fin 3 → Space :=\n  ![point 0 0 0, point 0 (a*T) T, point 0 (b*T) T]"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "upperVertices",
      "statement_lean": "def upperVertices (a b T : ℝ) : Fin 3 → Space :=\n  ![point 0 0 1, point (a*T) 0 (1-T), point (b*T) 0 (1-T)]"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "abbrev",
      "name": "Coord",
      "statement_lean": "abbrev Coord := Fin 2 → ℝ"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "standard",
      "statement_lean": "def standard : Set Coord := {x | 0 ≤ x 0 ∧ 0 ≤ x 1 ∧ x 0 + x 1 ≤ 1}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "vertices",
      "statement_lean": "def vertices : Fin 3 → Coord := ![0, ![1, 0], ![0, 1] ]"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "triangle",
      "statement_lean": "def triangle (a b c : E) : Set E := convexHull ℝ (range (![a, b, c] : Fin 3 → E))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "lowerAngularIndex",
      "statement_lean": "def lowerAngularIndex {N : ℕ} (j : Fin N) : Fin (2*N) :=\n  Fin.cast (two_mul N).symm (Fin.castAdd N j)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "upperAngularIndex",
      "statement_lean": "def upperAngularIndex {N : ℕ} (j : Fin N) : Fin (2*N) :=\n  Fin.cast (two_mul N).symm (Fin.natAdd N j)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "lowerAngularAxis",
      "statement_lean": "def lowerAngularAxis (ε : ℝ) (j : ℕ) : Space :=\n  let a := mesh (n ε) j\n  let b := mesh (n ε) (j+1)\n  unit (tiltV ε (alpha a b) (beta a b))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "upperAngularAxis",
      "statement_lean": "def upperAngularAxis (ε : ℝ) (j : ℕ) : Space :=\n  let a := mesh (n ε) j\n  let b := mesh (n ε) (j+1)\n  unit (tiltW ε (alpha a b) (beta a b))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "lowerAngularBase",
      "statement_lean": "def lowerAngularBase (ε : ℝ) (j : ℕ) : Set Space :=\n  let a := mesh (n ε) j\n  let b := mesh (n ε) (j+1)\n  let v := lowerVertices a b (cutoff ε a b)\n  projection (lowerAngularAxis ε j) '' triangle (v 0) (v 1) (v 2)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "upperAngularBase",
      "statement_lean": "def upperAngularBase (ε : ℝ) (j : ℕ) : Set Space :=\n  let a := mesh (n ε) j\n  let b := mesh (n ε) (j+1)\n  let v := upperVertices a b (cutoff ε a b)\n  projection (upperAngularAxis ε j) '' triangle (v 0) (v 1) (v 2)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "ExplicitAngularFamily",
      "statement_lean": "def ExplicitAngularFamily (ε : ℝ) (c : Fin (2*n ε) → TriCylinder) : Prop :=\n  ∀ j : Fin (n ε),\n    (c (lowerAngularIndex j)).axis = lowerAngularAxis ε j ∧\n    (c (lowerAngularIndex j)).base = lowerAngularBase ε j ∧\n    (c (upperAngularIndex j)).axis = upperAngularAxis ε j ∧\n    (c (upperAngularIndex j)).base = upperAngularBase ε j"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "AngularAreaStatement",
      "statement_lean": "def AngularAreaStatement : Prop :=\n  ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε ≤ 1 →\n    ∃ c : Fin (2*n ε) → TriCylinder,\n      ExplicitAngularFamily ε c ∧\n      totalArea c/H = ∑ j : Fin (n ε),\n        normalizedTerm ε (mesh (n ε) j) (mesh (n ε) (j+1)) ∧\n      |totalArea c/H - (1/2-(13/6000:ℝ)*ε^2)| ≤ 2*ε^4"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "ExplicitAngularStatement",
      "statement_lean": "def ExplicitAngularStatement : Prop :=\n  A_min = H ∧\n  ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε ≤ 1/2000 →\n    ∃ c : Fin (2*n ε) → TriCylinder,\n      ExplicitAngularFamily ε c ∧ Covers c ∧\n      |totalArea c/H - (1/2-(13/6000:ℝ)*ε^2)| ≤ 2*ε^4 ∧\n      totalArea c < A_min/2 ∧ relativeCost c < 1/2"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "abbrev",
      "name": "Plane",
      "statement_lean": "abbrev Plane := EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "abbrev",
      "name": "Space",
      "statement_lean": "abbrev Space := EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "vec2",
      "statement_lean": "def vec2 (x y : ℝ) : Plane := WithLp.toLp 2 ![x, y]"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "vec3",
      "statement_lean": "def vec3 (x y z : ℝ) : Space := WithLp.toLp 2 ![x, y, z]"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "axial",
      "statement_lean": "def axial (s : ℝ) (p : Plane) : Space := vec3 s (p 0) (p 1)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "IsParallelogram",
      "statement_lean": "def IsParallelogram {E : Type*} [AddCommGroup E] [Module ℝ E] (P : Set E) : Prop :=\n  ∃ c u v : E, LinearIndependent ℝ ![u, v] ∧\n    P = {x | ∃ s ∈ Icc (0 : ℝ) 1, ∃ t ∈ Icc (0 : ℝ) 1,\n      x = c + s • u + t • v}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "structure",
      "name": "Cylinder",
      "statement_lean": "structure Cylinder where\n  axis : Space\n  unit_axis : ‖axis‖ = 1\n  base : Set ((Submodule.span ℝ ({axis} : Set Space))ᗮ)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "carrier",
      "statement_lean": "def carrier (C : Cylinder) : Set Space :=\n  {x | ∃ b ∈ C.base, ∃ t : ℝ, x = (b : Space) + t • C.axis}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "area",
      "statement_lean": "def area (C : Cylinder) : ℝ := volume.real C.base"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "parallelogram",
      "statement_lean": "def parallelogram (C : Cylinder) : Prop := IsParallelogram C.base"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "openBounded",
      "statement_lean": "def openBounded (C : Cylinder) : Prop :=\n  IsOpen C.base ∧ Bornology.IsBounded C.base ∧ MeasurableSet C.base"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "familySet",
      "statement_lean": "def familySet {n : ℕ} (C : Fin n → Cylinder) : Set Space := ⋃ i, (C i).carrier"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "familyArea",
      "statement_lean": "def familyArea {n : ℕ} (C : Fin n → Cylinder) : ℝ := ∑ i, (C i).area"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "HasFiniteCover",
      "statement_lean": "def HasFiniteCover (shape : Cylinder → Prop) (S : Set Space) (A : ℝ) : Prop :=\n  ∃ n : ℕ, ∃ C : Fin n → Cylinder,\n    (∀ i, shape (C i)) ∧ S ⊆ familySet C ∧ familyArea C ≤ A"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "SmoothNear",
      "statement_lean": "def SmoothNear (n : WithTop ℕ∞) (D : Set Plane) (v : Plane → Plane) : Prop :=\n  ∃ U : Set Plane, IsOpen U ∧ D ⊆ U ∧ ContDiffOn ℝ n v U"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "HyperbolicAt",
      "statement_lean": "def HyperbolicAt (L : ℝ) (v : Plane → Plane) (p : Plane) : Prop :=\n  ∃ lam : ℝ, 0 < L * lam ∧ L * lam < 1 ∧\n    Module.End.HasEigenvalue (fderiv ℝ v p).toLinearMap lam ∧\n    Module.End.HasEigenvalue (fderiv ℝ v p).toLinearMap (-lam)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "weight",
      "statement_lean": "def weight (v : Plane) : ℝ := 1 / Real.sqrt (1 + ‖v‖ ^ 2)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "physicalWeight",
      "statement_lean": "def physicalWeight (h : ℝ) (w : Plane) : ℝ :=\n  1 / Real.sqrt (1 + w 0 ^ 2 + h ^ 2 * w 1 ^ 2)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "ruled",
      "statement_lean": "def ruled (D : Set Plane) (v : Plane → Plane) (L : ℝ) : Set Space :=\n  {z | ∃ p ∈ D, ∃ r : ℝ, |r| ≤ L ∧\n    z = vec3 (p 0 + r * v p 0) (p 1 + r * v p 1) r}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "physicalRuled",
      "statement_lean": "def physicalRuled (h : ℝ) (D : Set Plane) (v : Plane → Plane) (L : ℝ) : Set Space :=\n  {z | ∃ p ∈ D, ∃ s : ℝ, |s| ≤ L ∧\n    z = vec3 s (p 0 + s * v p 0) (h * (p 1 + s * v p 1))}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "HyperbolicStatement",
      "statement_lean": "def HyperbolicStatement : Prop :=\n  ∀ (D : Set Plane) (v : Plane → Plane) (L : ℝ),\n    IsCompact D → SmoothNear 1 D v → 0 < L →\n    (∀ p ∈ D, HyperbolicAt L v p) →\n    ∀ e : ℝ, 0 < e → HasFiniteCover Cylinder.parallelogram (ruled D v L)\n      ((∫ p in D, weight (v p)) + e)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "unitSquare",
      "statement_lean": "def unitSquare : Set Plane := {x | ∀ i, |x i| ≤ (1 : ℝ) / 2}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "IsSquareTile",
      "statement_lean": "def IsSquareTile (T : Set Plane) : Prop :=\n  ∃ c : Plane, ∃ R : Plane ≃ₗᵢ[ℝ] Plane, ∃ a : ℝ, 0 < a ∧\n    T = (fun x => c + a • R x) '' unitSquare"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "auxiliaryCylinder",
      "statement_lean": "def auxiliaryCylinder (T : Set Plane) (g : Plane) : Set Space :=\n  {z | ∃ s : ℝ, ∃ b ∈ T, z = axial s (b + s • g)}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "firstPhysicalMap",
      "statement_lean": "def firstPhysicalMap (h : ℝ) (z : Space) : Space := vec3 (z 0) (z 1) (h * z 2)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "secondPhysicalMap",
      "statement_lean": "def secondPhysicalMap (h a : ℝ) (z : Space) : Space :=\n  vec3 (z 1) (z 0) (h * (a - z 2))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "abbrev",
      "name": "Space",
      "statement_lean": "abbrev Space := EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "IsRegularTetrahedron",
      "statement_lean": "def IsRegularTetrahedron (K : Set Space) : Prop :=\n  ∃ v : Fin 4 → Space, AffineIndependent ℝ v ∧\n    ∃ edge : ℝ, 0 < edge ∧\n      (∀ i j : Fin 4, i ≠ j → dist (v i) (v j) = edge) ∧\n      K = convexHull ℝ (range v)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "shadowArea",
      "statement_lean": "def shadowArea (K : Set Space) (u : Space) : ℝ :=\n  TriangularCovering.area (TriangularCovering.projection u '' K)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "minimumShadowArea",
      "statement_lean": "def minimumShadowArea (K : Set Space) : ℝ :=\n  sInf {a : ℝ | ∃ u : Space, ‖u‖ = 1 ∧ a = shadowArea K u}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "triangularCovers",
      "statement_lean": "def triangularCovers {n : ℕ} (K : Set Space)\n    (C : Fin n → TriangularCovering.TriCylinder) : Prop :=\n  ∀ x ∈ K, ∃ i : Fin n, x ∈ (C i).carrier"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "triangularRelativeCost",
      "statement_lean": "def triangularRelativeCost {n : ℕ} (K : Set Space)\n    (C : Fin n → TriangularCovering.TriCylinder) : ℝ :=\n  ∑ i, TriangularCovering.area (C i).base / shadowArea K (C i).axis"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "relativeCost",
      "statement_lean": "def relativeCost {n : ℕ} (K : Set Space)\n    (C : Fin n → RuledApproximation.Cylinder) : ℝ :=\n  ∑ i, (C i).area / shadowArea K (C i).axis"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "AngularExplicitStatement",
      "statement_lean": "def AngularExplicitStatement : Prop :=\n  TriangularCovering.AngularAreaStatement ∧ TriangularCovering.ExplicitAngularStatement"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "RegularTriangularCounterexampleStatement",
      "statement_lean": "def RegularTriangularCounterexampleStatement : Prop :=\n  ∀ K : Set Space, IsRegularTetrahedron K →\n    ∃ n : ℕ, ∃ C : Fin n → TriangularCovering.TriCylinder,\n      triangularCovers K C ∧\n      TriangularCovering.totalArea C < minimumShadowArea K / 2 ∧\n      triangularRelativeCost K C < 1 / 2"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "RegularParallelogramCounterexampleStatement",
      "statement_lean": "def RegularParallelogramCounterexampleStatement : Prop :=\n  ∀ K : Set Space, IsRegularTetrahedron K →\n    ∃ n : ℕ, ∃ C : Fin n → RuledApproximation.Cylinder,\n      (∀ i, (C i).parallelogram) ∧\n      K ⊆ RuledApproximation.familySet C ∧\n      RuledApproximation.familyArea C < minimumShadowArea K / 2 ∧\n      relativeCost K C < 1 / 2"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "RegularOpenCounterexampleStatement",
      "statement_lean": "def RegularOpenCounterexampleStatement : Prop :=\n  ∀ K : Set Space, IsRegularTetrahedron K →\n    ∃ n : ℕ, ∃ C : Fin n → RuledApproximation.Cylinder,\n      (∀ i, (C i).openBounded) ∧\n      K ⊆ RuledApproximation.familySet C ∧\n      RuledApproximation.familyArea C < minimumShadowArea K / 2 ∧\n      relativeCost K C < 1 / 2"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "NilpotentC1Statement",
      "statement_lean": "def NilpotentC1Statement : Prop :=\n  ∀ (D : Set Plane) (G : Plane → Plane), IsCompact D → SmoothNear 1 D G →\n    (∀ b ∈ D, (fderiv ℝ G b).comp (fderiv ℝ G b) = 0) →\n    ∀ h eps : ℝ, 0 < h → 0 < eps →\n    ∃ n : ℕ, ∃ T : Fin n → Set Plane, ∃ g : Fin n → Plane,\n      (∀ i, IsSquareTile (T i)) ∧\n      physicalRuled 1 D G 1 ⊆ ⋃ i, auxiliaryCylinder (T i) (g i) ∧\n      (∑ i, volume.real (T i) * physicalWeight h (g i)) ≤\n        (∫ b in D, physicalWeight h (G b)) + eps ∧\n      ∀ a : ℝ, ∃ C₁ C₂ : Fin n → Cylinder,\n        (∀ i, (C₁ i).parallelogram ∧ (C₂ i).parallelogram ∧\n          (C₁ i).carrier = firstPhysicalMap h '' auxiliaryCylinder (T i) (g i) ∧\n          (C₂ i).carrier = secondPhysicalMap h a '' auxiliaryCylinder (T i) (g i) ∧\n          (C₁ i).area = h * volume.real (T i) * physicalWeight h (g i) ∧\n          (C₂ i).area = h * volume.real (T i) * physicalWeight h (g i))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "step",
      "statement_lean": "def step (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) : ℝ := (b - a) / N"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "point",
      "statement_lean": "def point (a b : ℝ) (N i : ℕ) : ℝ := a + i * step a b N"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "cell",
      "statement_lean": "def cell (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) (i : Fin N) : Set ℝ :=\n  Icc (point a b N i) (point a b N (i + 1))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "IsTagged",
      "statement_lean": "def IsTagged (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) (tag : Fin N → ℝ) : Prop :=\n  ∀ i, tag i ∈ cell a b N i"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "capMax",
      "statement_lean": "def capMax (R : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (N : ℕ) (i : Fin N) : ℝ :=\n  sSup (R '' cell a b N i)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "abbrev",
      "name": "Space",
      "statement_lean": "abbrev Space := TriangularCovering.Space"
    },

<a id="pm-s7"></a>
## 7. Complete mathematical paper


We prove that radially aligned segment sweeps admit finite cylinder covers with one triangular base for each interval of any tagged partition. As the mesh tends to zero, the total perpendicular base area converges to a weighted parameter area. An explicit application covers every regular tetrahedron with total base area below half its minimum projection area, giving negative answers to the half-area question and the directionwise normalized 1-Codimensional Cylinder Covering Conjecture.

<a id="pm-h-1"></a>
